135

Denford CNC 2600 Pro — London Hackspace

Identification lead

Main wiring diagram · SmoothieBox

Relay ratings below are corrected from Panasonic catalog pages 81–82; the main drawing now includes the current canonical source graph; fitted hardware remains unqualified.

Why this controller: Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate.

Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior STEP/DIR/ENABLE contacts feed one depicted SN74LVC07A interface per MSD556 axis. Retain the external drive power stages and qualify the spindle converter, command contacts and machine inputs separately.

Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate. Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior case contacts with retained external power stages. The main diagram and connection-ID schedule define the current proposed routes. Dotted GUESS routes need the named commissioning checks; OPEN routes remain disconnected until the missing terminal, rating or interface is established. Previous Core alternatives remain only in the superseded historical architecture record.

Current installation sequence
  1. Match the actual machine and controller revisions, trace the unpowered harness and verify all signal/reference, power and independent safety boundaries before energising. Follow the current connection IDs rather than the superseded architecture prose.
  2. Controller logic supply · separate from motor power: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Verify exact board supply path and rail ratings; motor current uses separate wiring and fuses.
  3. X external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  4. MSD556 X driver · supply and motor winding chain: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Never connect legacy +37 V to SmoothieBox or Prime logic/power. The mapped contact functions are motor-stage outputs; axis and individual cable identities remain unverified.
  5. Y external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  6. MSD556 Y driver · supply and motor winding chain: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Never connect legacy +37 V to SmoothieBox or Prime logic/power. The mapped contact functions are motor-stage outputs; axis and individual cable identities remain unverified.
  7. Z external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  8. MSD556 Z driver · supply and motor winding chain: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Never connect legacy +37 V to SmoothieBox or Prime logic/power. The mapped contact functions are motor-stage outputs; axis and individual cable identities remain unverified.
  9. Baldor VS1ST · SmoothieBox PWM to spindle speed analog input: Use the actual contact labels and per-wire qualification in the current main diagram. Any older Core/header reference in retained source context is not the selected controller endpoint.
  10. Baldor VS1ST · separate run contact, safety, power and spindle boundary: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Cabinet Wiring maps original J5 control-board pins11/12 to VS1ST 7/6 and says terminal1 +24 V is switched to terminal2 for drive enable. This is a reported original path, not proof of current cabinet wiring. Preserve physical E-stop, door/guard, contactor and relay chain. The spindle motor is on VS1ST U/T1,V/T2,W/T3 and PE, never on Smoothie; exact installed motor/line variant and terminal mapping require the nameplate/manual. See the separate fully named ULN2003AN/AQY212GS process-run proposal; no generic unspecified relay is needed.
  11. Denford limit, door and E-stop chain · do not repurpose from screenshot: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. The currently functional flag and retrofit plans do not prove which controller/relay revision is installed. The MRP-0850B drawing is Rev B while the machine may be Rev A; do not join or infer safety contacts from the wrong revision.
  12. Isolated run LED drive · power, bias and every IC pin: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New engineering proposal: SmoothieBox GPIO → ULN2003AN → AQY212GS LED, isolated contact pins3/4 to configured machine run circuit. This slow process interface does not implement E-stop, STO or PWM. Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.
  13. Isolated run contact · machine side only: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. These contact pins belong to the same U6 used in the preceding LED-drive panel. The optically isolated contact side has no conductor to SmoothieBox or ULN logic GND. Retain independent hardware stop/safety. Verify receiver mode and off-state leakage; this is a new unverified process-run proposal.
  14. Denford · retained guard, limit and brake boundaries · source revision check: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. These are individual source reference contacts, not a claim that the old controller is still fitted. Maintain the physical E-stop/guard/contactor chain. Z brake releases when energized; no unqualified SmoothieBox output may command brake or door unlock. Trace and document the current relay board, contact ratings and fail-safe behavior first. The separate VS1 run proposal is a process command and does not bypass this retained machine safety/enable chain.

One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Baldor VS1ST · SmoothieBox PWM to spindle speed analog inputOPA197 U3A + passive converter · pin 1 NC · leave unconnectedNo conductor drawn. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Baldor VS1ST MN767 Table 1-1 p.1-4: terminal 6 = analog input 1; 7 and 9 = common (internally connected); terminal 5 is +10 V ref but is not an output command source. Converter is a hand-built proposal using TI OPA197 SOIC-8 exact pins, R1 10kΩ/C1 10µF, Rf10k/Rg10k; it needs schematic review and bench test. OPA197 SOIC-8 NC pins 1, 5 and 8 are not connected. Fit local 100 nF bypass between U3 pins7 and4, plus nearby bulk capacitance chosen for supply impedance. R1/C1 time constant is 0.1 s: verify ripple and speed response at chosen PWM frequency. Sequence supplies so GPIO does not drive an unpowered stage; prove zero-command/startup output and loss-of-command shutdown. The case reference proposes 5V PWM rather than direct 3V3 GPIO: this selected converter uses gain 2. Verify actual output amplitude/polarity, rail tolerance, clipping and transfer with VFD disconnected; do not use the earlier gain-3 Core circuit on a 5V case signal.

Baldor VS1ST · separate run contact, safety, power and spindle boundaryVS1ST terminal strip · terminal1 +24VNo conductor drawn. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Cabinet Wiring maps original J5 control-board pins11/12 to VS1ST 7/6 and says terminal1 +24 V is switched to terminal2 for drive enable. This is a reported original path, not proof of current cabinet wiring. Preserve physical E-stop, door/guard, contactor and relay chain. The spindle motor is on VS1ST U/T1,V/T2,W/T3 and PE, never on Smoothie; exact installed motor/line variant and terminal mapping require the nameplate/manual. See the separate fully named ULN2003AN/AQY212GS process-run proposal; no generic unspecified relay is needed.

SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Cabinet Wiring maps original J5 control-board pins11/12 to VS1ST 7/6 and says terminal1 +24 V is switched to terminal2 for drive enable. This is a reported original path, not proof of current cabinet wiring. Preserve physical E-stop, door/guard, contactor and relay chain. The spindle motor is on VS1ST U/T1,V/T2,W/T3 and PE, never on Smoothie; exact installed motor/line variant and terminal mapping require the nameplate/manual. See the separate fully named ULN2003AN/AQY212GS process-run proposal; no generic unspecified relay is needed.

Isolated run LED drive · power, bias and every IC pinU5 · TI ULN2003AN · DIP16 · 9 · COM · NC (noninductive LED)No conductor drawn. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New engineering proposal: SmoothieBox GPIO → ULN2003AN → AQY212GS LED, isolated contact pins3/4 to configured machine run circuit. This slow process interface does not implement E-stop, STO or PWM. Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.

CircuitTerminalDisposition and sources
Baldor VS1ST MN767 August2008 single-phase manufacturer power reference · exclusive model-qualified branchBaldor VS1ST MN767 August2008 matching input variant only · L3 · unused in single-phase branchNo conductor drawn. Choose exactly one input branch after manufacturer model/nameplate verification. Source establishes terminal functions, not fitted installation. Protective earth is distinct from controller/analog/digital return. Supply and motor rating, wiring protection and individual fitted conductors remain commissioning prerequisites. MN767 uses U,V,W motor outputs, not Hitachi-style U/T1,V/T2,W/T3 captions. Motor ground goes to a drive ground terminal per printed4-1.

SmoothieBox Chapter 18 field-contact drawing; Baldor VS1ST MN767 August2008 manufacturer instruction manual

Earlier alternative retrofit architecture and circuit details

Owner retrofit guide · Denford CNC 2600 Pro

The Hackspace cabinet reference documents the original Baldor NextMove, MSD556 and VS1ST path, but the current controller is unresolved and its Rev B drawing may differ from the Rev A machine. Proposed Core-to-MSD routes preserve external driver stages; exact axis assignment is dotted pending motor tracing. This plan includes the actual MSD P1 common-anode inputs and P2 motor/power outputs and the full VS1ST 1–11 control strip schedule. Keep physical safety chain independent.

Which Smoothie target fits this machine?
  • SmoothieBox: Use a SmoothieBox with V2 Core for the existing MSD556 external stages. Verify the Core carrier exposes all STEP/DIR and logic return contacts; cabinet old Baldor/board interfaces should not be assumed to be direct harnesses.
  • V2 Core: Recommended signal controller if MSD556 drives remain. Two SN74LVC07A open-drain buffers can sink the specified MSD556 7–16mA input range within TI output ratings subject to guaranteed VOL conditions and correct machine +5V/logic return. Do not tie +37V to the Core.
  • V2 Prime: Prime can replace MSD556 stages only if every motor current/supply fits integrated driver ratings and cabinet architecture is intentionally changed; not a drop-in STEP/DIR adapter. Retaining MSD556 requires Core logic outputs plus buffers.
Retain or add
  • Retain each source-reported MSD556 V2.0 external stage and +37 V power supply if drive/motor ratings remain valid.
  • Two TI SN74LVC07A six-channel buffers: U1 for X/Y, U2 for Z for the three axes, 3.3V logic supply and local bypass capacitor; verify exact sink-current conditions and machine logic return.
  • One OPA197 U3 SOIC-8 + R1 10kΩ/C1 10µF/Rf20kΩ/Rg10kΩ per spindle analog proposal, or select a sourced isolated PWM-to-0–10V converter with matching PWM input.
  • Proposed run stage: TI ULN2003AN DIP16, Panasonic AQY212GS SOP4, 390 Ω LED resistor and 10 kΩ GPIO pull-down; separate PCB isolation layout required. Slow process command only; never substitute for safety chain.
Conversion and commissioning
  1. Check installed control board, machine version A/B, VFD and each MSD556 model labels; the wiki says the MESA/LinuxCNC retrofit is planned but does not confirm its completion.
  2. Trace each MSD556 to the motor; identify phase pairs by continuity, then map physical axes and verify current/microstep configuration.
  3. Wire X/Y Core channels to U1 and Z Core channels to U2 SN74LVC07A inputs; open drains go to the corresponding driver P1 negative inputs; tie P1 positive inputs to the new regulated +5 V opto distribution and establish a verified signal-current return. Confirm 7–16mA driver input against TI output current/VOL table.
  4. Keep P2 power/motor contacts separate: +37V supply to +V/GND; A+/A− and B+/B− to measured winding pairs. Never connect +37V to Core.
  5. Configure VS1ST analog terminal6/7 as speed reference and separate terminal2 DI1 using an isolated dry contact only after exact variant/input logic review. Maintain independent E-stop/door/contactor chain.
  6. Identify each endstop, Z brake, door/pump, and E-stop input/output from the fitted machine revision; commission one axis at a time with process spindle disabled.

Pin-by-pin schedule and commissioning notes
  • MSD556 Rev3.0 source P1 functions: 1 PUL+,2 PUL−,3 DIR+,4 DIR−,5 ENA+,6 ENA−; P2: GND,+V,A+,A−,B+,B−. 20–50VDC family range; 5.6A peak/4A RMS; input current 7–16mA; pulse ≤400kHz, pulse width >1.2µs, DIR setup ≥5µs; ENA typically unconnected/enabled.
  • SN74LVC07A SOIC-14 exact pins:1 1A,2 1Y,3 2A,4 2Y,5 3A,6 3Y,7 GND,8 4Y,9 4A,10 5Y,11 5A,12 6Y,13 6A,14 VCC; open-drain outputs accept up to 5.5V. Confirm input sink-current requirement against guaranteed TI VOL/current conditions.
  • Baldor VS1ST MN767 Table1-1 p.1-4: 1 +24V,2 digital input1,3 digital input2,4 analog input2/digital input3,5 +10V ref,6 analog input1 0–10V,7 common,8 analog output 0–10V,9 common (connected to7),10 relay common,11 relay NO (250VAC/6A or 30VDC/5A).
  • Core P1 J5 .16/.18,.22/.24,.28/.30,.34/.36 are four STEP/DIR channels; .13 is shared MOT_EN, not per-axis enable. Grounds .19,.20,.25,.26,.32,.37,.38,.39; J35.6 PWM, .7 TTL candidate, .10 GND. Slash net names are namespace labels, not polarity evidence.
  • Machine reference contact inventory: original Baldor J1–J3 (11), J5–J9 (22), relay/power board J1/J8/J10–13 (26), VS1ST 1–11 (11), selected MSD contacts (10), 80 source-reference contacts/17 groups. Actual controller/driver mapping, mating face, axis assignments and many E-stop/limit contacts unresolved.

Complete wiki-researched machine dossier

Denford CNC 2600 Pro — London Hackspace

Wiki evidence status: machine-specific page identifies a Denford 2600 Pro. The wiki records a planned MESA/LinuxCNC conversion and says the installed version differed from a linked “Design B” schematic; commissioning status is therefore unresolved.

Machine identity and use boundaries

London Hackspace identifies the model as “2600 pro,” donated to the space. The page says it is in the woodshop and limits the machine to non-metal materials; liquid and mist coolant are prohibited. ER20 collets are used and users supply their own tooling. The page lists dust extraction as unresolved/planned and marks induction as TBD.

Controller and documented retrofit state

The page's next steps list installing a MESA board and configuring LinuxCNC. Other headings list MESA 7i76E manuals/connection material and schematics, but one note warns that the provided Design B schematic may differ from the installed Version A. The page also mentions Baldor VS1ST Micro-Series VFD and MSD556 V2.0 stepper-drive documentation, and says the original controller was a Baldor Nextmove. These records do not establish that the MESA conversion was completed.

Pinout evidence

The wiki links an E-stop relay diagram, cabinet wiring and a Design B schematic, and explicitly warns of a possible A/B revision mismatch. Since the actual installed revision and complete diagrams were not independently read in this pass, no terminal or connector pinout is transcribed. The planned MESA I/O must not be treated as commissioned or wired from a linked manual alone.

Source

  • Denford CNC 2600 — London Hackspace Wiki — exact model, material restrictions, ER20 collets, controller/retrofit notes, and schematic-revision warning.
  • Category:Equipment — London Hackspace Wiki — catalogue corroboration of “Denford CNC 2600.”

Unknowns: installed controller now, completion of the MESA/LinuxCNC retrofit, Version A cabinet wiring, and any verified pin-by-pin I/O assignment.

Wiki sources

Sources are restricted to the supplied wiki-research dossier. External vendor/forum links are not used as evidence.

  2. Category:Equipment — London Hackspace Wiki

Baldor VS1ST MN767 · conditional input and PE reference

For a matching VS1ST, the August 2008 manual’s printed pages 4-2–4-5 (PDF pages 16–19) show single-phase input at L1/L and L2/N; the three-phase branch adds L3. Motor outputs are U, V and W, with protective-earth connections shown at both input and motor sides. Choose only the input branch matching the fitted drive nameplate; these drawings do not identify the installed unit or its wiring.

Verify the nameplate supply class, motor and protective-earth path; preserve existing mains protection and the independent stop circuit. No supply rating or conductor assignment is inferred here. The current main includes the canonical conditional interface circuits; the complete manufacturer VFD alternatives remain supplementary references. Drive identity, mains supply class, protective earth and fitted wiring remain unqualified.

Sources: captured local manual · MN767, PDF pp. 16–19; Drive Centre mirror of the manufacturer-authored manual · MN767.
